    export let namedEntityTests(): List<TokenizerTest> {
      let all = new ListBuilder<TokenizerTest>();
      let rows = namedEntityTestsText().split("\u{E001}");
      for (var i = 0; i < rows.length; i += 1) {
        let f = rows[i].split("\u{E002}");
        all.add(named(f[0], f[1], f[2]));
      }
      all.toList()
    }
